requirement has not yet been formalized; it must not be inferred from roadmap prose.

the actual adjoint action | The analytic core is now constructed for continuous finite complex matrix representations: normalized-Haar averaging is integrable and invariant, and strict positivity is derived from continuity, nonzero identity value, and positive Haar measure of nonempty open support. Conjugate symmetry, sesquilinear laws, exact real self-norm identity, nonnegativity, and definiteness now package the average as a named `InnerProductSpace.Core` without replacing the carrier's existing norm. The coordinatewise Reynolds average `∫σ(g⁻¹)Aρ(g)` is also constructed and proved to be an exact intertwiner by right-Haar substitution; The average is bundled as Mathlib's exact `IntertwiningMap`; algebraic Schur gives a bijective-or-zero dichotomy, and inequivalent irreducible coordinate representations force the entire averaged rectangular matrix to zero. Algebraic closedness of `ℂ` and Mathlib's endomorphism Schur theorem now prove every irreducible self-average is a named complex scalar multiple of identity. Cyclicity of trace and normalized-Haar mass one prove conjugation averaging preserves trace; hence `n·c_A=tr(A)` and positive dimension gives `c_A=tr(A)/n`. The unitary inverse/conjugate-entry bridge is derived separately from exact matrix unitarity and representation inversion. Applying the normalized Schur formulas to exact matrix units now proves all-index self orthogonality `n⁻¹δδ` and zero mixed coefficients for explicitly inequivalent irreducible unitary representations. Finite diagonal summation through genuine integrable Haar coefficients also proves norm one for every represented positive-dimensional irreducible unitary character and zero pairing for explicitly inequivalent irreducible characters. With the exact inverse-entry convention, the representation-valued Fourier transform of `ρ(g)ₐᵦ` at `ρ` is the forced transposed matrix unit `n⁻¹Eᵦₐ`, while its transform at an explicitly inequivalent irreducible is zero; a hostile probe rejects the untransposed replacement. Finite coefficient synthesis is now packaged as a linear map whose transform is exactly `n⁻¹Aᵀ`; it is injective, its range is linearly equivalent to the matrix space, and the coefficient block has exact dimension `n²`. The same finite block now satisfies `⟨f_A,f_B⟩=n⁻¹⟨A,B⟩ₕₛ=n⟨f̂_A(ρ),f̂_B(ρ)⟩ₕₛ`; a hostile probe shows that dropping the dimension weight forces the represented block to be one-dimensional. Finite pairwise-inequivalent irreducible character families now have continuous central synthesis, exact coefficient analysis and coordinate pairing, injectivity, and exact span dimension equal to the index cardinality. Finite pairwise-inequivalent full coefficient families are also packaged with dependent representation dimensions: whole-block transforms vanish at inequivalent members, analysis recovers `dᵢ⁻¹Aᵢᵀ`, synthesis is injective, and the exact range dimension is `∑ᵢdᵢ²`. A generic analysis lemma identifies pairing against a synthesized block with the Hilbert–Schmidt pairing against the transposed Fourier coefficient. It yields the exact finite-family formulas `∑ᵢdᵢ⁻¹⟨Aᵢ,Bᵢ⟩ₕₛ` and `∑ᵢdᵢ⟨f̂_A(ρᵢ),f̂_B(ρᵢ)⟩ₕₛ`, with genuine finite integration. Trace characters and basis-dependent coefficients, including normalized-Haar orthogonality, now transport across explicitly equivalent presentations in the separate quotient/Fourier chain. The averaged real pairing is packaged as a continuous bilinear map in the original finite coordinate topology; finite-dimensional positive-ellipsoid coercivity gives exact complex von Neumann boundedness, and Mathlib's core comparison theorem proves that the Haar-averaged norm induces exactly the original topology, including dimension zero. The topology-compatible normed-additive, complex-normed, and inner-product structures are now exposed as named locally installable values; their inner product is exactly the averaged pairing. Every group matrix is packaged as an exact linear equivalence with inverse at `g⁻¹`, and its action preserves the named averaged norm. A noncomputably selected exact orthonormal basis of the averaged realization is now reindexed by the original `Fin n` coordinate size. Its ordinary linear coordinate equivalence sends each selected basis vector to the exact Kronecker coordinate and identifies the averaged pairing with the standard coordinate Hermitian pairing. Conjugating the original action through this exact coordinate equivalence now constructs a genuine continuous matrix representation with the explicit `U ρ(g) U⁻¹` formula and a `Representation.Equiv` back to the original coordinates. Pairing invariance is transported to the standard coordinate pairing; a reusable column/Kronecker theorem then derives the exact matrix equation `star σ(g) * σ(g) = 1`. This completes finite-coordinate Haar unitarization without assuming Peter–Weyl. A reusable group-algebra module equivalence now proves irreducibility invariant under exact representation equivalence. Consequently every explicit positive-dimensional continuous irreducible matrix representation—without a supplied unitary law—constructs an equivalent continuous irreducible unitary coordinate representative and hence a coordinate-unitary-dual class; already-unitary bundles recover their original class. The invariant Haar-orthogonal complement of every subrepresentation is now constructed and proved an exact algebraic complement; this gives semisimplicity and a finite simple group-algebra decomposition. Every selected simple summand now has exact scalar-restricted finite coordinates, continuity, irreducibility, and an explicitly equivalent Haar-unitarized matrix representative. Exact Kronecker tensor products, explicit standard row-major `Fin (n*m)` flattening, coefficient multiplication, continuity, unitarity, and finite simple unitary-coordinate decomposition are also derived. Exact finite analysis/synthesis now returns every tensor-coefficient product to a finite sum through the selected simple group-algebra summand actions; exact representation-equivalence coordinates now expand those action terms into finite weighted sums of selected positive-dimensional continuous irreducible unitary matrix coefficients. The exact contragredient representation and unitary inverse-entry law now expand pointwise scalar stars into finite weighted sums of selected irreducible unitary coefficients. Constants and this finite coefficient span are now bundled as an exact `StarSubalgebra ℂ C(G, ℂ)` whose multiplication and star closure come respectively from tensor and contragredient reconstruction. Point separation and density remain open. Realization of arbitrary non-coordinate abstract representations, all-irreducible indexing, infinite-series summability, and completeness remain open. Hall's argument does not by itself extend existence to every generic manifold representation API |

`HasAbelianGroupLieAlgebra` and `HasProperNonzeroGroupLieIdeal` are precise mutation predicates for
the hostile suite, not alternative admissibility conditions. The project currently has no concrete
inhabitant of `CompactSimpleGaugeGroupData`: Mathlib supplies algebraic matrix `SU(n)` but not the
needed manifold, compactness, connectedness, tangent identification, and simplicity chain. This is
open positive-consistency infrastructure, not an existence claim about Yang–Mills theory.

Every designated principal chart now supplies a smooth local section and a tangent lift proved right-inverse to the actual projection differential. Horizontality proves tangent-lift independence at each fixed total-space point, and right adjoint equivariance proves equality in the actual adjoint quotient across right-related representatives. A generic pointwise dependent-fiber base form is constructed and proved presentation-independent under horizontality/equivariance. The pointwise descent is specialized to the exact derived curvature and its indexed structure certificate. The quotient-derived fiber topology is now proved compatible with the transported additive and real-module structures through named locally installed topological-module values. A reusable order-indexed carrier records continuous derivative slots and two alternating curvature slots in the exact dependent fiber; only order zero is constructed, definitionally from the same smoothly descended curvature. Positive-order curvature-derivative tensors and their recursive connection law remain absent; this statement is about that tensor hierarchy, not the separately derived conditional intrinsic `D_A F = 0` theorem. Exact local lifts preserve smooth base tangent fields, have explicit smooth chart-local ambient extensions, and support smooth principal-form evaluation. Freed's paper concerns Chern–Simons theory; only its general §1 connection geometry is used here.
It is not a source for the Yang–Mills action, quantum existence, or a mass gap.
`PrincipalBundleTorsorData.trivial` is positive consistency evidence for the algebraic fiber
interface. Hostile probes reject empty/missing fibers, base-moving or law-breaking actions,
nonidentity stabilizers, and nontransitive fibers. Algebraic map/gauge probes reject projection
mismatch, failed equivariance, base movement, and noninjective automorphisms; the identity and
inverse laws provide positive checks. Topological probes reject discontinuous projection/action,
uncovered or out-of-atlas charts, malformed chart domains/codomains, and failed local equivariance;
the product bundle is a concrete positive model. The smooth layer requires manifold structures,
smooth projection/action, and both smooth directions for every atlas chart; the smooth product
bundle is a positive model. `principalBundleOverlapDomain`, `principalBundleTransition`, and
`SmoothPrincipalBundleData.transition_smoothOn` package the derived smooth overlap map; its domain
is open, its first coordinate is fixed, and reversing the ordered transition is proved to recover the
original coordinate. Smooth gauge automorphisms require both forward and inverse smoothness
and retain projection/equivariance laws. General smooth bundle maps and curvature remain open rather
than being hidden in a placeholder proposition; the smooth connection-form definition is staged
below. Local sections derived from the selected trivializations prove the projection is an open
quotient map.

`ManifoldDifferentialForm I M V k` is reusable mathematics: a pointwise family of continuous
alternating `k`-linear maps on tangent spaces with values in a real topological module whose
addition and scalar action are continuous. Its pullback is defined only with an explicit smooth
map proof and preserves degree. The carrier intentionally does **not** claim that the family varies
smoothly with the base point; no exterior derivative is built into this pointwise carrier. The
separate certified one-form interface is described above. Zero and alternation probes prevent the
pointwise carrier from being mistaken for the completed connection/curvature API.
`SmoothManifoldDifferentialForm` adds local, coordinate-respecting regularity by requiring smooth
evaluation on every tuple of locally smooth tangent fields, after an explicit continuous-linear
value-coordinate bridge. `isSmooth_iff_valueCoordinates` proves independence from the chosen
continuously linearly equivalent normed value model. The zero form gives positive consistency
evidence.

`PointwisePrincipalConnectionData` remains useful staging infrastructure: orbit and right-translation
derivatives are taken only after their maps are proved smooth, vertical normalization forces
infinitesimal freeness, and equivariance uses the separately proved `Ad(g⁻¹)` action.
`PrincipalConnectionData` promotes that exact form with `SmoothManifoldDifferentialForm.IsSmooth`;
its `toSmoothForm` bridge is definitionally coherent. The hostile equivariance theorem is a
requirement-presence guard; a concrete example separating `Ad(g⁻¹)` from `Ad(g)` awaits a concrete
nonabelian group witness. No concrete connection form is supplied yet.

separate/same-witness/all-coordinate sequence-existence probes. | The Lean result exactly retains a continuous faithful finite complex matrix representation as data. Earlier finite complete reducibility places each of its entries in the irreducible-unitary coefficient star subalgebra; Mathlib's complex Stone–Weierstrass theorem then yields closure `⊤`. Basis-aware equivalence transport and an explicit trivial class transfer density to the selected quotient-dual synthesis; the reverse inclusion identifies its range exactly with the all-presentation coefficient span, yielding the named general continuous density target; normalized-Haar regularity and Mathlib continuous-function `L²` density derive the existing normalized-Haar coordinate-dual completeness target; dense-range Hilbert arguments then yield finite-support approximation at every positive tolerance and coefficient-test uniqueness. Hall's explicit conjugation-average/Schur step is separately represented by `CompactGroupCharacterCentralizationData`; combined with full density it yields both central targets. The actual normalized-Haar averaging operator is now constructed under explicit second countability and proved continuous, central, norm-nonincreasing, surjective, idempotent, and identity on central functions. The exact formula is now proved: coefficient synthesis is `tr(ρAᵀ)`, averaging leaves the existing Schur average, and normalized Schur trace yields the inverse-dimension trace character weight. Direct-sum induction constructs the full datum, so second-countable full density—and hence faithful finite matrix coordinates—now conditionally inhabit both central targets. Noncanonical finite-support character choices at tolerance `1/(n+1)` then give proved uniform and normalized-Haar `L²` approximation sequences. Exact `L²` character analysis has norm one, recovers every finite coordinate, equals `∫conj(χ_q)f` on continuous-central inputs, and proves each chosen approximant coordinate converges to that integral. The selected character vectors are orthonormal; Bessel gives unconditional square summability and vectorwise countable coefficient support, while finite synthesis has exact Parseval. Every finite class set has a bounded orthogonal projection with exact truncation, idempotence, finite-support inversion, residual orthogonality, Pythagorean remainder, contraction, and nonempty norm one. The unconditional finite-subset net converges exactly on the closed character span, with exact `tsum` Parseval; central completeness characterizes convergence on the central surrogate and conditionally yields continuous-central integral Parseval. Independently, the sharp uniform character norm equals dimension, and weighted `Summable (‖a_q‖dim(q))` gives an unconditional uniformly convergent continuous-central series with exact pointwise/identity `tsum` and Weierstrass control. Haar analysis recovers every weighted coefficient, weighted support is countable, and weighted synthesis is injective. Direct matrix-coefficient orthogonality also gives `χ_q⋆χ_r=δ_{qr}dim(q)⁻¹χ_q` in the fixed convolution order. Bounded linear convolution passes both explicitly weighted unconditional uniform sums to derive `series(a)⋆series(b)=series(a_qb_qdim(q)⁻¹)` without an unjustified infinite sum/integral exchange. The caller-supplied Casimir spectral coefficients therefore satisfy the conditional positive-time law `K_s⋆K_t=K_{s+t}`, with no time-zero identity or heat-kernel status. The explicit selected trivial class plus the geometric bridge separately yield conditional normalized-Haar mass one. Separate uninhabited real-valuedness/strict-positivity data conditionally produce a positive normalized with-density probability measure, explicitly without selecting Fourier partial sums or asserting inversion for arbitrary inputs. These are conditional compact matrix-group theorems, not a proof that every compact Hausdorff group has one faithful finite representation, dual countability, unrestricted infinite inversion, or a full general Peter–Weyl theorem. |

`∫f(g)ρ(g⁻¹)`, avoiding an artificial matrix norm. Continuity and compactness derive genuine coefficient integrability, and matrix trace is proved equal to the scalar coefficient against the inverse trace character. Lévy's cited passage motivates the central-character/Peter–Weyl destination but does not itself fix this project-level representation-valued Fourier convention. The exact Driver/Sengupta complex convolution order is also defined, packaged on `C(G,ℂ)` under the explicit second-countability needed for parametric integration, bounded by `‖f‖∞‖g‖∞`, and exposed as bounded linear maps after fixing either ordered input. Compact-product integrability, Fubini, left-Haar substitution `z=x*y`, inverse product order, and finite coordinate separation derive `(f⋆g)̂(ρ)=ĝ(ρ)f̂(ρ)` without commutativity. Irreducible matrix-coefficient/character orthogonality and exact finite-family algebraic Plancherel are now derived in the separate Hall/Schur chain. The coordinate unitary dual quotient now covers every explicitly bundled positive-dimensional continuous irreducible unitary matrix representation and proves distinct selected classes inequivalent. Its dependent direct sum packages finite support over all coordinate classes; exact Fourier analysis proves `f̂_A(q)=d_q⁻¹A(q)ᵀ`, synthesis injectivity, and the justified algebraic inversion `A(q)=d_q f̂_A(q)ᵀ` on that range. Additive direct-sum pairings then prove the all-coordinate-class algebraic Plancherel identity `∑qd_q⁻¹⟨A(q),B(q)⟩ₕₛ=∑qd_q⟨f̂_A(q),f̂_B(q)⟩ₕₛ`; these are finite sums because the first family has direct-sum support. A separate smooth coordinate quotient now stores exact `ContMDiff` matrix coordinates and maps injectively into the continuous quotient. Its image is exactly `HasSmoothRepresentative`; surjectivity is equivalent to every continuous class having such a representative and is not asserted. The algebraic synthesis is now mapped into Mathlib's actual normalized-Haar `Lp ℂ 2`; its `L²` inner product is exactly the algebraic Fourier pairing. The algebraic range, its closed span, and `HasL2PeterWeylCompleteness` (`closedSpan = ⊤`, equivalently density) are concrete. The separately source-traced faithful compact matrix-group theorem now constructs a conditional inhabitant after selected-representative transport and normalized-Haar regularity; no general compact-group inhabitant is constructed. The all-coordinate-class character specialization now has finitely supported scalar coefficients, continuous central synthesis, exact Haar coefficient recovery, injectivity, and orthonormal coordinate pairing. Linear-trace conjugation proves that explicitly equivalent coordinate presentations have the same character and that each quotient-selected character agrees pointwise with every bundled representative of its class; raw matrix coefficients remain basis-dependent. The exact rectangular coordinate matrices of every supplied representation equivalence and its inverse are constructed, proved mutually inverse on their respective coordinate spaces, and used to derive the full `E ρ(g) E⁻¹` conjugation and double-sum coefficient transport formula. Finite-sum/integral exchange and the existing self-orthogonality formula then derive the exact mixed normalized-Haar pairing for two explicitly equivalent presentations, including both conjugated coordinate-change factors; the identity equivalence recovers the self formula. The exact central continuous carrier and finite character synthesis are now constructed, and `HasCentralContinuousPeterWeylDensity` names their compact-group uniform-density relation with finite-approximant semantics; Hall centralization now conditionally inhabits it from second-countable full coefficient density, including the faithful finite matrix-group case, but not for arbitrary compact groups. The exact finite character pairing is now also realized as the actual normalized-Haar `L²` inner product; every selected character vector and analysis functional has norm one, analysis recovers synthesized coordinates, and its value on a continuous central function is the exact integral `∫conj(χ_q)f`. The full selected character family is orthonormal in normalized-Haar `L²`; finite and unconditional Bessel inequalities give square summability and vectorwise countable support (not global dual countability), and finite synthesis satisfies exact norm-square/`tsum` Parseval. Every finite class set has a bounded orthogonal projection with exact coordinate truncation, idempotence, support inversion, residual orthogonality, Pythagorean remainder, contraction, and nonempty norm one. The unconditional finite-subset net converges exactly on the closed character span with Parseval; central completeness is equivalent to convergence on the project's central surrogate and conditionally gives continuous-central integral Parseval. Independently, the exact uniform character norm equals dimension, and weighted `Summable (‖a_q‖dim(q))` yields an unconditional uniformly convergent continuous-central series with pointwise/identity `tsum` and Weierstrass control. Haar analysis recovers every weighted coefficient, each weighted support is countable, and weighted synthesis is injective. Direct matrix-coefficient orthogonality gives the exact normalized-Haar law `χ_q⋆χ_r=δ_{qr}dim(q)⁻¹χ_q` in the fixed convolution order. The same coordinate calculation proves that an arbitrary raw coefficient convolved with a matching trace character is inverse-dimension times the unchanged row/column coefficient and vanishes against an inequivalent character; quotient-selected branching retains the original basis. Bounded linear convolution extends this extraction through every weighted uniform character series, yielding unconditional diagonal Casimir heat action and the uniform-norm right zero-time generator on each explicit coefficient. Bounded linear convolution operators pass both explicitly weighted unconditional uniform sums through convolution and derive `series(a)⋆series(b)=series(a_qb_qdim(q)⁻¹)` without an unjustified infinite sum/integral exchange. The caller-supplied Casimir spectral coefficients consequently satisfy the conditional positive-time convolution law `K_s⋆K_t=K_{s+t}` in the uniform norm and pointwise, without a time-zero identity or heat-kernel status. A separate uninhabited geometric bridge requires every selected character to satisfy `Δχ_q=-c_qχ_q` for the unchanged pairing Laplacian, proves weight uniqueness, the exact coefficient derivative, and the single-term `∂ₜ=½Δ` identity without infinite-series differentiation. Heat-trace summability at `t/2` additionally yields weighted unconditional uniform convergence of the exact coefficient-derivative series at `t`, but no differentiation interchange. The uniformly convergent formal Laplacian series is exactly twice the derivative candidate; uninhabited interchange data separately require spatial Laplacian passage and time differentiation and only then derive `∂ₜK_t=½ΔK_t`. The explicit selected trivial class has character/dimension one; a geometric bridge forces its weight to zero and exact character analysis yields conditional normalized-Haar mass one. Separate uninhabited real-valuedness/strict-positivity data conditionally produce a positive normalized with-density probability measure. An uninhabited positive-time heat-trace summability datum now conditionally constructs Lévy's exact `dim(q)exp(-(t/2)c_q)` uniformly convergent central spectral series, pointwise/identity formulas, norm control, time-addition coefficient law, Haar analysis, and derived dual countability—without yet identifying the weights with the geometric Laplacian or claiming a heat kernel. Continuity also proves every coordinate of the noncanonical finite approximation sequence converges to that integral without asserting completeness or an infinite synthesis identity. Finite characters and continuous central functions now have exact normalized-Haar `L²` ranges and closed spans; `HasCentralL2PeterWeylCompleteness` names their equality, follows from uniform central density, and has finite-approximant semantics, and is conditionally inhabited in the same second-countable full-density/faithful matrix-group cases, but not at arbitrary compact-group generality. This reusable target generalizes Lévy's standing compact connected Lie-group and §4.2.2 semisimple-group scope to arbitrary compact topological groups. Its closed continuous-central image span is explicitly a project surrogate for Lévy's literal central square-integrable carrier, not a proved identification with an AE-central subspace. The selected continuous synthesis range is now proved exactly equal to the all-presentation finite coefficient span, and its density is named `HasContinuousPeterWeylDensity`; faithful compact matrix groups satisfy it and it implies the `L²` target, but the general compact-group target remains open. Thus the continuous quotient is still not identified with Lévy's full smooth compact-Lie-group dual; no all-abstract-representation coordinate realization, comparison surjectivity, countability, unconditional/general Peter–Weyl density/completeness proof, unrestricted infinite-series inversion, Casimir theorem, or heat-kernel expansion is claimed |

Dimensions one through three remain consistency regimes requested by this project, not claims in the
Clay problem statement. Existing general sources support the uninhabited current-strength `d=3`
specialization; any positive lower-dimensional construction or stronger physical contract requires
separate declaration-level provenance. The `d=4` current-strength core is an incomplete integration
surface at the actual Clay dimension. Its new universal wrapper has the correct outer gauge
quantifier and existential carrier packaging but remains explicitly `CurrentStrength`, uninhabited,
and not the final acceptance proposition.

## Signature-foundation decisions

| Lean declaration | Meaning | Source / decision | Classification | Hostile evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `EuclideanDimension.CoordinateVector` | Algebraic real coordinate vectors with no selected topology or form | Project definition over the dimension index | Definition | Forms are added separately rather than hidden in the carrier |
| `EuclideanDimension.euclideanQuadraticForm` | All-positive sum-of-squares form | Standard mathematical convention; Clay p. 5 distinguishes Euclidean spacetime but does not state this formula | Formalization definition | `negative_euclidean_value_blocked` |
| `EuclideanDimension.minkowskiQuadraticForm` | Mostly-minus form with coordinate zero positive | Clay p. 5 §3, text 227–232 requires Minkowski signature and translation generators; `(+,-,…,-)` is an explicit project convention | Formalization definition | `nonpositive_minkowski_time_basis_blocked`, `nonnegative_minkowski_spatial_basis_blocked` |
| `EuclideanDimension.spatialIndexSucc` | Places spatial indices after coordinate zero | Project coordinate convention; no reconstruction asserted | Definition | spatial-basis negative-value theorem |
| `euclideanQuadraticForm_nondegenerate`, `minkowskiQuadraticForm_nondegenerate` | Both selected forms have zero radical | Derived from Mathlib's radical theorem for weighted sums of squares and the nonzero weights | Derived theorem | `degenerate_euclideanQuadraticForm_blocked`, `degenerate_minkowskiQuadraticForm_blocked` |
| `one_euclideanQuadraticForm_eq_minkowskiQuadraticForm` | In `d = 1`, no spatial weight distinguishes the two algebraic forms | Derived from the definitions; does not identify theories | Derived theorem | exact proof |
| `euclideanQuadraticForm_ne_minkowskiQuadraticForm` | From `d = 2` onward, a spatial basis vector separates the forms | Derived from the definitions | Derived theorem | 2D and 4D identification-blocking probes |

No Lorentz group, Euclidean group, analytic continuation, or OS bridge is claimed by these
quadratic-form declarations.

## Non-source project declarations

| Lean declaration | Purpose | Authority | Axiom status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `YangMills.Audit.audit_yang_mills_axioms` command | Kernel-level rejection of `sorryAx` and project-defined axioms | Project validation infrastructure; uses `Lean.collectAxioms` | Checked by the root build and mutation fixtures |

Every future canonical physical declaration must be added here in the same commit that introduces
it. “Pending” rows are obligations, not accepted placeholder fields.
